# Novel 2- and 3-word sequences in the L papers

Every 2- and 3-word prose sequence occurring in L1-L7, minus (a) every tuple
that also occurs in any of the 68 PDFs in `papers/`, (b) 92 tuples that are
accepted mathematical terminology. Math (inline and display), LaTeX commands,
and punctuation break sequences, so no tuple spans a formula or a clause end.
Counts are total occurrences across the seven papers; letters name the papers.

Bigrams 4636, trigrams 6839, total 11475.
